**Additional 2026-07-11 — Plan 242's current ship candidate has been
revalidated in the worktree.**
The code/docs contract is stable: guest-executed runtime binaries are
centralized into the shared read-only cache artifact under
`~/.cache/mvm/runtime-overlay/<version>/<arch>/`; every admitted backend/tier
either mounts that artifact read-only with real proof or refuses the
unsupported path fail-closed; stopped VMs pick up newer version-matched
overlays on restart, while running VMs keep the runtime they booted with. The
cache now carries a recorded `checksums-sha256.txt`, and required-overlay
boots re-verify those cached bytes before attach. The current closeout slice
also fixes the exact-current candidate witnesses by
aligning the SDK/fixture receipt posture tests with the current vsock-only
libkrun contract and isolating the `mvm-build` library tests from shared
env/cache races. Fresh host gates are green on the current candidate, and the
Linux-native `cargo test -p mvm-build --lib --offline` witness is green on
`88.99.197.234` from a fresh source snapshot. The rollout/runbook surface is
explicit in the docs and release note. Branch/PR synchronization is an
external closeout step and is not part of the implementation status recorded
here.

Older entries below are retained as the execution log. Where they describe
earlier intermediate gaps or proofs-in-progress, this top entry is the
authoritative final state for Plan 242.

Read any later "remaining gap", "still open", or "not yet proven" wording as
point-in-time historical context only; those statements are superseded by the
final state above where Plan 242 is concerned.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now fail-safes the native Linux builder default while the rootfs-backed libkrun builder lane is still not proven.** The remaining production gap is the Linux/KVM libkrun builder path, not the read-only runtime-overlay contract on workload backends. `crates/mvm-build/src/builder_backend_select.rs` therefore no longer auto-detects libkrun on `LinuxNative`; it now resolves `LinuxNative -> qemu`, keeps `macOS 26+ Apple Silicon -> hvf`, and leaves other hosts on libkrun, while explicit `--builder libkrun` / `MVM_BUILDER_BACKEND=libkrun` continue to work for continued diagnosis. The same slice updates CLI help, doctor text, `rootfs.rs`, and the contributor development guide so the operator-facing contract matches the code. Validation in the worktree: `env MVM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-data CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-target cargo test -p mvm-build --features builder-vm builder_backend_select::tests::auto_detect_default_for_linux_native_picks_qemu --lib --locked --offline -- --exact --nocapture`, `env MVM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-data CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-target cargo test -p mvm-build --features builder-vm builder_backend_select::tests::auto_detect_default_for_non_linux_non_hvf_hosts_picks_libkrun --lib --locked --offline -- --exact --nocapture`, `env MVM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-data CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-target cargo check -p mvm-cli --locked --offline`, and `env MVM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-data CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-target cargo clippy -p mvm-build --features builder-vm --lib --tests --locked --offline -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-10 — Plan 242 now fail-closes explicit Linux rootfs-backed libkrun builder attempts based on a minimal real-host repro.** The default flip alone still left explicit `--builder libkrun` / `MVM_BUILDER_BACKEND=libkrun` able to walk into the same dead rootfs-backed boot shape. `crates/mvm-build/src/libkrun_builder.rs` now refuses the steady-state cached rootfs builder image on `LinuxNative` with `BuilderVmError::LibkrunUnavailable(...)` and points the operator at the qemu builder instead, while leaving root-dir/bootstrap mode available. The guard is backed by a narrower host experiment on `88.99.197.234`: a tiny ext4 image with a trivial `/init` and `/dev/console` stayed completely silent under the libkrun rootfs launch (`console.log` stayed zero bytes), while the matching root-dir launch on the same host immediately reached guest console and emitted `Couldn't execute '/init' inside the vm: No such file or directory`. That is enough to pin the remaining broken seam to Linux libkrun `rootfs_path` mode itself rather than the kernel or the root-dir path. Validation in the worktree: `env MVM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-data CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-target cargo test -p mvm-build --features builder-vm libkrun_builder::tests::linux_native_rootfs_builder_support_guard_matches_platforms --lib --locked --offline -- --exact --nocapture`, `env MVM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-data CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-target cargo test -p mvm-build --features builder-vm libkrun_builder::tests::ensure_builder_vm_image_accepts_current_manifest_capabilities --lib --locked --offline -- --exact --nocapture`, `env MVM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-data CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-target cargo check -p mvm-build --features builder-vm --all-targets --locked --offline`, and `env MVM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-data CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-target cargo clippy -p mvm-build --features builder-vm --lib --tests --locked --offline -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-10 — Plan 242 now removes the last active SSH fallback from the legacy Firecracker builder orchestrator.** The supported builder/runtime contract is direct vsock now, but `crates/mvm-build/src/pipeline/orchestrator.rs` had still kept a reachable SSH builder backend and an `auto -> vsock then SSH fallback` branch. The active path now stays on the surviving transport only: `MVM_BUILDER_MODE=vsock` remains the explicit FC builder path, `auto` is only a legacy alias for that same direct-vsock path, and the old `MVM_BUILDER_AUTHORIZED_KEY` / authorized_keys injection surface is gone from active builder-artifact preparation and the public docs. Validation in the worktree: `env MVM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-verify-data MVM_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-verify-cache CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-verify-target cargo check -p mvm-build --offline`, `env MVM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-verify-data MVM_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-verify-cache CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-verify-target cargo test -p mvm-build --offline test_ensure_builder_artifacts_skips_when_present test_ensure_builder_artifacts_downloads_when_missing -- --nocapture`, and `env MVM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-verify-data MVM_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-verify-cache CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-verify-target cargo clippy -p mvm-build --all-targets --offline -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 HVF workload read-only proof is now green locally too.** The remaining backend-proof umbrella gap for required-overlay block-root workloads is closed. The exact OCI witness now passes on macOS/HVF on the production workload seam: `MVM_EMBED_ZIG=/Users/auser/.local/share/mise/installs/python/3.12.10/lib/python3.12/site-packages/ziglang/zig MVM_OCI_REQUIRED_OVERLAY_SMOKE=1 MVM_OCI_IMAGE_RUNNER_HYPERVISOR=hvf MVM_DATA_DIR=/private/tmp/mvm-oci-hvf-data-fixed2 MVM_CACHE_DIR=/private/tmp/mvm-oci-hvf-cache-fixed2 CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/private/tmp/mvm-oci-hvf-target-fixed2 cargo test --test oci_image_runner_smoke run_image_block_root_required_overlay_is_read_only_on_selected_backend -- --exact --nocapture`. Two fixes made that witness pass: guest `mvm-verity-init` now detects whether the verity sidecar carries a superblock and chooses `hash_start_block=1` or `0` by on-disk geometry, and the injected OCI `/init` now skips remounting `/mvm/runtime` when the verity init already mounted the overlay. `OCI_RUNTIME_EPOCH` also moved to `5` so stale injected OCI roots rematerialize with the corrected contract. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-guest --bin mvm-verity-init -- --nocapture`, `cargo test -p mvm-build init_script_ --lib -- --nocapture`, `cargo check -p mvm-cli --all-targets`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-build -p mvm-cli -p mvm-guest --all-targets -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now fixes one concrete libkrun required-overlay boot mismatch instead of only sharpening diagnostics.** The required-overlay libkrun workload path had still been reusing the generic verity cmdline contract from Firecracker/qemu/HVF, which points PID 1 at `/dev/vda` + `/dev/vdb` for the sealed rootfs pair and `/dev/vdc` + `/dev/vdd` for the runtime overlay pair. That is wrong for libkrun's initramfs-only boot shape: `KrunContext::add_disk()` numbers its first extra disk as `/dev/vdb` when `rootfs_path` is absent. `crates/mvm-backend/src/libkrun.rs` now emits a libkrun-specific required-overlay cmdline with root data/hash on `/dev/vdb` + `/dev/vdc` and runtime data/hash on `/dev/vdd` + `/dev/vde`, and focused tests pin that mapping in the built `SupervisorConfig`. Validation in the worktree: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-backend-libkrun-target cargo test -p mvm-backend libkrun_build_supervisor_config --lib -- --nocapture`, `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-backend-libkrun-target cargo test -p mvm-backend libkrun_verity_cmdline_args_shift_devices_for_initrd_boot --lib -- --nocapture`, `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-backend-libkrun-target cargo check -p mvm-backend --all-targets`, and `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-backend-libkrun-target cargo clippy -p mvm-backend --all-targets -- -D warnings`. This does not close the libkrun live proof row yet; the real-host witness on `88.99.197.234` still needs a rerun against binaries that carry the corrected cmdline.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 libkrun workload read-only proof is now green on the real KVM host.** After syncing the corrected libkrun verity cmdline into `/root/mvm-overlay-required-plan-live/`, the exact OCI required-overlay witness for the libkrun workload row now passes on `88.99.197.234`: `MVM_OCI_REQUIRED_OVERLAY_SMOKE=1 MVM_OCI_IMAGE_RUNNER_HYPERVISOR=libkrun CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-libkrun-target cargo test --test oci_image_runner_smoke run_image_block_root_required_overlay_is_read_only_on_selected_backend -- --exact --nocapture`. That is the previously missing guest-visible closeout for the libkrun sealed block-ext4 row: a real libkrun guest reaches userspace with `mvm.runtime_source_policy=required_overlay`, sees `/mvm/runtime` mounted read-only, and the guest-side write probe fails with `Read-only file system`. At this point in the rollout log the qemu/HVF rows still needed their own live proofs; those later proofs landed and are reflected in the final state note above.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 qemu workload read-only proof is now green on the real KVM host too.** The same backend-neutral OCI required-overlay witness also now passes against `qemu` on `88.99.197.234`: `MVM_OCI_REQUIRED_OVERLAY_SMOKE=1 MVM_OCI_IMAGE_RUNNER_HYPERVISOR=qemu CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-libkrun-target cargo test --test oci_image_runner_smoke run_image_block_root_required_overlay_is_read_only_on_selected_backend -- --exact --nocapture`. That closes the qemu sealed block-ext4 workload row with guest-visible evidence, not only host-side attach tests: the OCI guest reports `/mvm/runtime` mounted read-only under `mvm.runtime_source_policy=required_overlay`, and the write probe fails with `Read-only file system`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now removes the zero-byte embedded host-binary trap from source-checkout proof lanes.** The shared boot-host-binary resolver in `mvm-cli` now distinguishes between release-style embedded payloads and source checkouts that intentionally carry zero-byte stubs. In the latter case it source-builds the host-side `mvm-build` binaries once with `cargo zigbuild`, caches them under the host-bins cache, and feeds both Stage 0 `/init` bytes and `/mvm-bins` from that cache. The Stage 0 root-dir bootstrap, workload-kernel compile path, builder-image cache loader, default-microVM local build path, and HVF builder-rootfs injector now all use that resolver instead of bailing on `embedded stage0-init is a zero-byte stub`. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-cli --test host_binaries_extract -- --nocapture`, focused unit tests `host_binaries::extract::tests::runtime_host_target_dir_honors_cargo_target_dir` and `...::source_built_dir_is_workspace_scoped`, `cargo check -p mvm-cli`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-cli -- -D warnings`. Real-host follow-on on `88.99.197.234` confirms the failure boundary advanced: the exact OCI required-overlay witness no longer dies on embedded stubs and instead reaches a real Stage 0 build, where the open blocker is now later builder fetch connectivity (`github.com ... via 127.0.0.1`) on the all-vsock builder path.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now moves the local HVF/libkrun proof lane past a worktree-specific bootstrap trap.** The source-checkout builder-image bootstrap helper no longer hard-codes `<workspace>/target/mvm-builder-vm-bootstrap`; it now honors `CARGO_TARGET_DIR`, which lets isolated worktree/local proof runs keep helper artifacts in the same writable target root as the parent test invocation. The same `builder-vm` lane was also updated for the current three-argument `KrunContext::new` signature so the focused regressions compile again. This is not a claim that the HVF read-only proof is closed; it is a proof-enablement slice. On the local macOS host the ignored HVF witness now fails later on real environment prerequisites (default-home cache/audit writes, then Stage 0 nix-seed download DNS once `MVM_DATA_DIR` / `MVM_CACHE_DIR` are redirected into `/tmp`) instead of the old `<worktree>/target/.../.cargo-lock` path bug. Validation in the worktree: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-hvf-proof-target cargo test -p mvm-build --features builder-vm libkrun_builder::tests::builder_vm_bootstrap_helper_target_dir_is_dedicated --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-hvf-proof-target cargo test -p mvm-build --features builder-vm libkrun_builder::tests::builder_vm_bootstrap_helper_target_dir_honors_cargo_target_dir --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-hvf-proof-target cargo test -p mvm-build --features builder-vm libkrun_builder::tests::builder_vsock_egress_switches_stage0_launches_to_vsock_direct --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, and `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-overlay-hvf-proof-target cargo clippy -p mvm-build --features builder-vm --lib --tests -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now removes the same shared-target drift from the guest/runtime binary build path itself.** The source-checkout cross-build path in `crates/mvm-build/src/guest_agent_build.rs` no longer assumes `<workspace>/target/<triple>/release` for guest-agent and runtime-overlay binaries. `GuestAgentBuildSpec` now carries an optional `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` override, the zigbuild invocations export it when present, and `output_dir()` resolves under that override instead of the shared workspace target root. This is another proof-enablement slice rather than a backend closeout claim: it keeps worktree-local runtime-overlay rebuilds from silently reusing sibling worktrees' stale guest binaries while the read-only overlay rollout is still converging. Validation in the worktree: `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-guest-build-target cargo test -p mvm-build guest_agent_build::tests::output_dir_defaults_to_workspace_target_when_env_absent --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-guest-build-target cargo test -p mvm-build guest_agent_build::tests::output_dir_honors_cargo_target_dir_env --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-guest-build-target cargo test -p mvm-build guest_agent_build::tests::build_argv_targets_musl_with_dev_shell_and_both_bins --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, and `CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-guest-build-target cargo clippy -p mvm-build --lib --tests -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now exposes the runtime-overlay build debt as an explicit command instead of a hidden boot-time tax.** The same shared attach/acquire path used by required-overlay boots is now available directly as `mvmctl build runtime-overlay build`, with `--source auto|build|download` and `--force`, plus a worktree-safe `just runtime-overlay-build` wrapper through `bin/dev`. That means contributors can prebuild or refresh the version-matched read-only runtime overlay once, then iterate on the rest of the system without each later required-overlay boot rediscovering that guest-binary build on the hot path. Validation in the worktree: `cargo check -p mvm-cli`, `cargo test -p mvm-cli commands::build::runtime_overlay::tests::runtime_overlay_build_subcommand_parses --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, `cargo test -p mvm-cli commands::build::runtime_overlay::tests::requested_acquire_mode_honors_explicit_source --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, `cargo test -p mvm-cli commands::tests::build_runtime_overlay_subcommand_parses --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-cli --lib --tests -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now roots the direct-path runtime-overlay tool caches under `MVM_CACHE_DIR` too.** The same source-checkout build path no longer relies on host-global cache defaults like `~/Library/Caches` while assembling guest/runtime binaries. `crates/mvm-build/src/guest_agent_build.rs` now exports `CARGO_ZIGBUILD_CACHE_DIR=<MVM_CACHE_DIR>/cargo-zigbuild` and `ZIG_GLOBAL_CACHE_DIR=<MVM_CACHE_DIR>/zig`, creates those directories before spawning `cargo zigbuild`, and leaves `HOME` / `CARGO_HOME` / `RUSTUP_HOME` alone. The explicit operator command therefore now completes end to end with isolated state: `MVM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/mvm-runtime-overlay-data MVM_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/mvm-runtime-overlay-cache CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-runtime-overlay-target cargo run -- build runtime-overlay build` produced the sealed `runtime-overlay/0.17.0/aarch64/overlay.{ext4,verity}` artifact and the matching `cargo-zigbuild/` + `zig/` cache trees under `MVM_CACHE_DIR`. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-build guest_agent_build::tests::zigbuild_cache_dir_lives_under_mvm_cache_dir --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, `cargo test -p mvm-build guest_agent_build::tests::apply_zigbuild_env_exports_explicit_cache_dir --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, `cargo check -p mvm-build`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-build --lib --tests -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now aligns the builder-VM bootstrap helper with that same source-checkout stub policy.** The temporary `mvm-builder-vm-bootstrap` helper used to refresh an empty local builder-image cache no longer hard-forces `MVM_EMBED_BINARIES=1`, which had dragged local HVF/libkrun proof runs back onto the older pinned-Zig embedded-host-binary path. `crates/mvm-build/src/libkrun_builder.rs` now builds that helper with `MVM_SKIP_EMBED_BINARIES=1` and clears `MVM_EMBED_BINARIES`, relying on the already-landed `ensure_boot_host_binaries(...)` source-build path when the helper later needs real musl host binaries. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-build --features builder-vm libkrun_builder::tests::bootstrap_helper_build_command_uses_stub_embed_mode --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, `cargo test -p mvm-build --features builder-vm libkrun_builder::tests::builder_vm_bootstrap_helper_target_dir_honors_cargo_target_dir --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, `cargo check -p mvm-build --features builder-vm --all-targets`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-build --features builder-vm --all-targets -- -D warnings`. Local macOS/HVF follow-on confirms the failure boundary advanced again: the ignored `builder_runner::hvf_builder::tests::live_hvf_builder_runtime_overlay_is_read_only` witness now reaches a real Stage 0 nix-seed fetch and stops on external DNS/network access rather than the old embedded-helper `zig 0.13.0 is required` trap.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 closes two more source-checkout direct-path gaps without claiming backend closeout.** First, the source-built boot-host-binary fallback now includes an explicit `BOOTSTRAP_SUPPORT_BINARIES` set, so Stage 0's pre-overlay `/mvm-bins` cache carries `mvm-egress-client` for the all-vsock egress path instead of silently omitting it. Second, the raw-HVF auxiliary-binary resolver now honors `CARGO_TARGET_DIR` when looking for and returning source-built helpers like `mvm-hvf-supervisor`, eliminating the isolated-worktree case where the helper build succeeded under `/tmp/...` and the resolver still reported it missing. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-cli host_binaries::extract::tests::host_binary_names_include_bootstrap_support_binaries --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, `cargo test -p mvm-backend aux_bin::tests::source_candidate_check_honors_cargo_target_dir --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, `cargo test -p mvm-backend aux_bin::tests::workspace_target_bin_dirs_honor_cargo_target_dir --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, plus package `check`/`clippy` runs for `mvm-cli` and `mvm-backend`. Local macOS/HVF follow-on narrows the remaining ignored live-proof blocker again: the read-only overlay witness now reaches raw HVF boot and stops on `hvf boot failed: BadKernel`, so the next open seam is the HVF builder-image/kernel bootstrap assumption rather than the host-binary cache or helper resolver.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now closes the local HVF production seam too.** The last two local blockers on the actual CLI HVF builder path are gone. The source-built boot-host-binary fallback now groups requested binaries by owning package before invoking `cargo zigbuild`, so `mvm-egress-client` is rebuilt from `mvm-guest-helpers` instead of failing under the old single-package `mvm-build` assumption. The HVF boot path now also reserves and allows the fifth virtio-blk slot the overlay-enabled builder needs, eliminating the immediate `BadKernel` preflight on the runtime-overlay builder shape. The live read-only witness now sits on the production CLI seam (`resolve_hvf_builder_image()` + `HvfBuilderVm`) and passes locally with isolated cache/data dirs. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-cli host_binaries::extract::tests::host_binary_build_groups_include_guest_helpers_support_binary --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, `cargo test -p mvm-backend hvf::kernel_boot::tests::fifth_disk_slot_stays_below_virtiofs_window --lib -- --exact --nocapture`, package `check`/`clippy` for both crates, and the live macOS/HVF command `MVM_EMBED_ZIG=... CARGO_TARGET_DIR=/tmp/mvm-cli-hvf-image-target MVM_DATA_DIR=/tmp/mvm-local-hvf-data-iso-2 MVM_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/mvm-local-hvf-cache-2 cargo test -p mvm-cli commands::build::hvf_builder_image::tests::live_resolved_hvf_builder_runtime_overlay_is_read_only --lib -- --ignored --exact --nocapture`, which passed. The only remaining nuance is worktree operational isolation: concurrent worktrees sharing the default builder-store lock under `~/.cache/mvm` can block one another, so local proof runs should isolate `MVM_CACHE_DIR` when another builder process is active.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now keeps persistent OCI required-overlay boots on the workload-kernel lane too.** The detached OCI block-root path no longer decides between dev/prod kernel fallback purely from the machine profile while the runtime contract is resolved separately. `start_persistent_oci_machine` now treats `RuntimeSourcePolicy::RequiredOverlay` as sufficient to force the prod/workload kernel lane, even when the machine profile is `dev`, so a runtime-lean verity-backed root cannot silently reuse a dev-tier kernel cache fallback while still claiming the read-only overlay contract. This is a drift-closure slice, not a libkrun closeout claim: the remaining live blocker is still later guest execution under libkrun. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-cli runtime_source_policy_for_workload_boot_tests --lib --locked` and `cargo clippy -p mvm-cli --lib --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now makes the remaining libkrun workload blocker self-describing on the same all-vsock seam.** The workload-side libkrun timeout is no longer a bare `supervisor did not bind vsock socket ...` error that forces a second manual inspection pass on the host. `LibkrunBackend::start` now appends the VM state dir, the current `vsock-*.sock` inventory, and tail/status output for `supervisor.lifecycle.log`, `supervisor-config.json`, and `console.log` whenever the guest-agent socket never appears. That does not claim the libkrun closeout is finished; it sharpens the next real-host rerun by telling us directly whether the guest never reached userspace or whether it reached userspace but still failed to bind `vsock-5252.sock`, all without reintroducing any gateway-era transport assumptions. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-backend persist_supervisor_config_dump_writes_workload_json_dump --lib --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-backend libkrun_startup_diagnostics_reports_vsock_and_log_state --lib --locked`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-backend --lib --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now closes a stale shared workload-selector seam too.** The current CLI persistent OCI workload path already routes sealed block-ext4 `libkrun`/`hvf`/`qemu` boots through `RequiredOverlay`, but the shared `mvm::machine::Machine::to_start_config` helper was still discarding backend/sealed/root-strategy context and defaulting back to the old fallback posture. `LaunchInputs` now carries those selectors and threads them through the same `select_runtime_source_policy(...)` matrix the command layer uses, so a non-CLI machine launch cannot silently drift off the rollout contract while the all-vsock read-only overlay work continues. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm to_start_config_requires_overlay_for_sealed_libkrun_block_workloads --lib --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm to_start_config_keeps_virtiofs_roots_on_rootfs_only --lib --locked`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm --lib --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now tightens the OCI persistent block-root path around the same required-overlay contract.** Three local drift points are closed in the same worktree. First, `resolve_or_pull_run_image` now self-heals cached OCI block roots when `rootfs.verity` / `rootfs.roothash` are missing, not only when `rootfs.ext4` itself vanished. Second, `start_persistent_oci_machine` no longer treats `sealed: true` as the only route to `RequiredOverlay`: a runtime-lean OCI block root with verity sidecars now qualifies for the required-overlay matrix even though its `mvm-meta.json` stays dev-accessible (`sealed: false`). That path also now probes the rootfs verity sidecars into `VmStartConfig` instead of leaving persistent OCI workloads on an ext4-only boot shape by default. Third, the same persistent OCI path now resolves an effective verity initrd for required-overlay boots, preferring a sibling `rootfs.initrd` and otherwise falling back to a dedicated host-built `verity-initrd/<version>/<arch>/rootfs.initrd` cache entry assembled from the cached `mvm-verity-init` guest binary. The installed-binary path now reaches the same cache through embedded `mvm-verity-init` bytes, and source checkouts populate it through the existing host-side guest-binary cross-compile path, so this recovery step no longer depends on a Nix-built default image. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-cli rootfs_verity_sidecars_present_requires_both_files --lib --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-cli resolve_run_image_missing_rootfs_without_unpacked_layers_asks_for_repull --lib --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-cli runtime_source_policy_for_workload_boot_tests --lib --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-build verity_initrd --lib --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-cli default_microvm_tests --lib --locked`, `cargo clippy -p mvm-build --lib --locked -- -D warnings`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-cli --lib --locked -- -D warnings`. Real-host follow-on on `88.99.197.234` narrows the remaining live gap further again: the synced checkout now rewrites the OCI cache entry into a verity-backed runtime-lean block root, no longer stops on "required-overlay ... needs initrd", no longer detours through Stage-0/Nix recovery, and instead reaches the later libkrun guest-agent socket timeout with a real VM state dir under `/root/.mvm/vms/libkrun-workload-proof12/`, `dispatch_route: legacy_direct_vsock`, `networking=VsockDirect`, `console.log` still empty, and only `vsock-5251.sock` present. The remaining blocker is therefore back in libkrun guest startup, not initrd acquisition or source-checkout artifact recovery.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now cleans up the operator-facing all-vsock contract in the same worktree.** The active runtime-overlay rollout no longer tells contributors or operators that `gvproxy`, `passt`, or another guest-NIC gateway binary is still required on the supported builder/runtime paths. `mvmctl doctor`'s `network-backend` row now reports the real contract directly (`direct vsock only; no host gateway binary is part of the active runtime contract`), the builder-egress summary drops the stale `gvproxy gateway` wording, and the contributor development guide removes the old gateway-install instructions for supported macOS/Linux builders. This keeps the live user-facing story aligned with the already-landed `VsockDirect` transport cleanup while leaving archival ADR history untouched. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-cli builder_egress_lease_is_ok_and_names_ip --lib --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-cli check_struct_reports_ok --lib --locked`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-cli --lib --tests --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now gives the libkrun workload path the same host-visible config breadcrumb as the builder lane.** `LibkrunBackend::start` now persists the exact `SupervisorConfig` JSON it hands `mvm-libkrun-supervisor` into `<vm_state_dir>/supervisor-config.json` before spawn, and the backend has a focused unit test pinning that file path + JSON shape. This is a proof-enablement slice, not a closeout claim: the remaining libkrun live blocker is still post-config guest execution. But it gives the next real-host compare step a direct "known-good workload config" artifact on the same host as the failing builder witness, which removes more guesswork from the builder-vs-workload diff. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-backend persist_supervisor_config_dump_writes_workload_json_dump --lib --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-backend libkrun_build_supervisor_config_uses_verity_shape_and_runtime_overlay_tokens --lib --locked`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-backend --lib --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now moves the libkrun workload failure boundary past the old bridge refusal too.** `mvm-libkrun-supervisor` now routes admitted `VsockDirect` workloads through `legacy_direct_vsock` instead of forcing them into `run_supervisor_with_bridge`, which explicitly rejects no-NIC direct VMs. On `88.99.197.234`, the rerun of `mvmctl machine run --hypervisor libkrun --name libkrun-workload-proof --image docker.io/library/alpine:3.20 -d` no longer dies in `configure_with_gateway_for_bridge refuses VsockDirect`; `supervisor.lifecycle.log` now records `dispatch_route: legacy_direct_vsock`, and the host instead times out waiting for `vsock-5252.sock` with zero console bytes while leaving a workload-side `supervisor-config.json`. That narrows the remaining libkrun gap again: workload and builder rootfs-backed lanes now both fail later, after direct-vsock dispatch selection, instead of hiding behind the old bridge-route incompatibility. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-vm-host --bin mvm-libkrun-supervisor --features libkrun-sys bridge_route_skips_admitted_vsock_direct_workloads --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-vm-host --bin mvm-libkrun-supervisor --features libkrun-sys bridge_route_keeps_gateway_backed_admitted_workloads --locked`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-vm-host --bin mvm-libkrun-supervisor --features libkrun-sys --locked -- -D warnings`; real-host evidence captured under `/root/.mvm/vms/libkrun-workload-proof/`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now exposes an earlier builder userspace witness on the same all-vsock seam in the same worktree.** The overlay-backed libkrun builder lanes no longer wait until the late single-shot dispatch response to prove the guest reached `/mvm/runtime`. `mvm-build::libkrun_builder` now registers `mvm_guest::vsock::GUEST_AGENT_PORT` whenever the read-only runtime-overlay rootfs contract is active, and persistent-builder start waits for that socket before reporting ready. This does not claim the remaining libkrun live proof is closed, but it gives the libkrun/HVF closeout a backend-neutral “PID 1 mounted the runtime overlay and forked `/mvm/runtime/agent`” witness on the same vsock-only control surface. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-build --features builder-vm builder_runtime_overlay_guest_agent_only_enables_for_rootfs_overlay_path --lib --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-build --features builder-vm builder_runtime_overlay_attachment_uses_read_only_disk_for_rootfs_images --lib --locked`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-build --features builder-vm --lib --tests --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now proves the remaining libkrun builder gap is later than port registration too.** A real-host rerun against the synced worktree and the already-fixed cache (`MVM_CACHE_DIR=/tmp/mvm-builder-cache-initfix`) shows the live `supervisor-config.json` now carries the expected `vsock_ports: [21471, 5252]` for the builder-dispatch and overlay-backed guest-agent seams, while `networking=VsockDirect` stays intact. But the VM state dir and `/proc/net/unix` still only ever expose `vsock-5253.sock`; neither `vsock-21471.sock` nor `vsock-5252.sock` appears before the guest goes silent. That removes the last ambiguity about “maybe the new socket was never requested” and leaves the open libkrun closeout squarely in post-config guest execution / host-socket materialization. Evidence captured on `88.99.197.234` under `/tmp/mvm-builder-cache-initfix/builder-vm/vms/mvm-builder-vm-1783621678744-564942/` after syncing the current `libkrun_builder.rs`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now proves the remaining libkrun builder gap is not the port mix, transport-disk fanout, or the sealed `ro` cmdline either.** Follow-on hand-run `mvm-libkrun-supervisor` experiments on `88.99.197.234` reused that same fixed cache and varied only the builder image shape: `vsock_ports=[21471,5252]`, `vsock_ports=[5252]`, `vsock_ports=[21471]`, a runtime-overlay-only disk layout with `vsock_ports=[5252]`, and the same runtime-overlay-only layout with a workload-style `root=/dev/vda rw init=/init` cmdline. Every variant stayed silent: zero guest console bytes and no `listen=true` host socket in the per-VM state dir. That leaves the open work deeper in libkrun’s execution of this builder rootfs shape, not in the requested socket topology we hand it.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now removes the stale libkrun gateway selector from the active builder/runtime path in the same worktree.** The overlay rollout no longer models `gvproxy`, `passt`, or `rvproxy` as if they were still valid transport choices for the libkrun builder lane. `mvm-build::libkrun_builder` now resolves libkrun transport to `VsockDirect` unconditionally, a non-empty `MVM_NETWORKING` only emits a stale-compatibility warning instead of switching gateways, and `LibkrunNetworkProvider` now tags the active path as `vsock_direct`. This keeps the code aligned with the all-vsock architecture without overstating the remaining proof gap: the live libkrun builder witness is still blocked, but now specifically by the guest shutting down immediately after libkrun wires the explicit vsock device. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-build --lib libkrun_builder --features builder-vm --locked` and `cargo clippy -p mvm-build --lib --features builder-vm --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now tightens the builder overlay contract across backend families in the same worktree.** The shared backend-agnostic builder spec no longer advertises `prefer_overlay` once a read-only runtime-overlay disk is attached: disk-backed builder lanes now emit `required_overlay`, matching the actual all-vsock, read-only overlay topology already used by the libkrun builder path. This narrows the remaining exception to the explicit root-dir/bootstrap lanes instead of leaving a contradictory fallback policy in the common spec. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-backend builder_runner --lib --locked`, `cargo check -p mvm-backend --lib --locked`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-backend --lib --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now persists earlier libkrun builder breadcrumbs in the same worktree.** `mvm-host-vm-init` no longer waits for `/nix-store` before it has any host-visible lifecycle trace: once the `job` share is mounted, breadcrumb lines are mirrored into `/job/mvm-host-vm-init.lifecycle.log` as well as the in-guest `/run` log. That gives the remaining live libkrun witness a concrete way to distinguish "guest never reached PID 1" from "PID 1 reached virtiofs/job and then died before the persistent store mount" on the next real-host rerun. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-build --bin mvm-host-vm-init --locked`, `cargo check -p mvm-build --bin mvm-host-vm-init --locked`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-build --bin mvm-host-vm-init --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now surfaces the new lifecycle evidence in the shared builder diagnostics too.** The backend-neutral missing-`/job/result` path now includes both `supervisor.lifecycle.log` from the VM state dir and `mvm-host-vm-init.lifecycle.log` from the host job dir, so libkrun/qemu/HVF builder failures report whether the guest reached the `job` share before dying without requiring a second manual inspection pass on the host. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-build builder_vm_runtime --lib --locked`, `cargo check -p mvm-build --lib --locked`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-build --lib --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now aligns the libkrun builder kernel-format contract with the workload backend in the same worktree.** The rootfs-backed libkrun builder now normalizes x86_64 kernels through the existing `ensure_fc_loadable_kernel` helper and passes the resulting explicit `KernelFormat` into `KrunContext` instead of inheriting libkrun's `Raw` default. That removes a plausible earliest-boot mismatch from the remaining libkrun proof surface and keeps the builder launch recipe aligned with the already-working workload libkrun path. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-build --lib libkrun_builder --features builder-vm --locked`, `cargo check -p mvm-build --lib --features builder-vm --locked`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-build --lib --features builder-vm --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now surfaces and narrows the libkrun live-proof blocker directly in the same worktree.** The remaining live read-only proof gap on the libkrun builder path is still real, but it is no longer hidden behind a bare "missing /job/result" ENOENT. `mvm-build::builder_vm_runtime` now enriches missing-result failures with the builder VM state dir plus `console.log` / `supervisor.{stdout,stderr}.log` status and short tails, and the libkrun/qemu/HVF shell-job paths all use that shared helper. Follow-on diagnostics in the same worktree now also make the supervisor write `supervisor.lifecycle.log` under the VM state dir, and the checked-in `libkrun-sys` wrapper now has an explicit `VsockDirect` mode that disables libkrun's implicit vsock transport before adding the explicit vsock device. Local validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-build builder_vm_runtime --lib --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-backend hvf_builder --lib --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-vm-host --bin mvm-libkrun-supervisor --features libkrun-sys --locked`, `cargo test -p libkrun-sys --lib --features libkrun-sys --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-build --lib libkrun_builder --features builder-vm --locked`, `cargo clippy -p mvm-build -p mvm-backend --lib --locked -- -D warnings`, `cargo clippy -p mvm-vm-host --bin mvm-libkrun-supervisor --features libkrun-sys --locked -- -D warnings`, and `cargo clippy -p libkrun-sys -p mvm-build --lib --features builder-vm --locked -- -D warnings`. Real-host validation on `88.99.197.234`: rerunning the ignored `libkrun_builder::tests::live_libkrun_builder_runtime_overlay_is_read_only` witness in `/root/mvm-overlay-required-plan-live/` still fails before `/job/result`, but the failure now proves the blocker has moved. The early lifecycle reruns showed `console.log`, `supervisor.stdout.log`, and `supervisor.stderr.log` all present but empty under `/root/.cache/mvm/builder-vm/vms/mvm-builder-vm-1783605350472-60525/`, the forced-rebuild follow-on under `/root/.cache/mvm/builder-vm/vms/mvm-builder-vm-1783605706935-103145/` also proved there was no builder-dispatch vsock response within 5 seconds of supervisor exit, and the lifecycle-breadcrumb rerun under `/root/.cache/mvm/builder-vm/vms/mvm-builder-vm-1783606162682-108933/` proved the supervisor reached `dispatch_config` and `run_legacy` with `networking=Tsi`. After the `VsockDirect` cutover, the latest real-host reruns under `/root/.cache/mvm/builder-vm/vms/mvm-builder-vm-1783607052663-150009/` and `/root/.cache/mvm/builder-vm/vms/mvm-builder-vm-1783607171633-153409/` prove the supposed vsock-only builder lane is no longer inheriting libkrun's default networking mode: `supervisor.lifecycle.log` now records `networking=VsockDirect`, and a trace-enabled rerun shows libkrun wiring the balloon/rng/console/fs/block/vsock devices before immediately reporting `Received KVM_EXIT_SHUTDOWN signal`, still with zero console bytes and no builder-dispatch response. The remaining blocker is therefore later than transport selection: the no-TSI direct path is accepted, but the guest still shuts down before PID 1 finalizes.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now narrows one shared guest-runtime contract divergence with the transparent-net work too.** The host-side egress-CA cmdline encoder now emits the compact `mvm.egress_ca=pem:<body>` token instead of a hex-encoded full PEM, and both the OCI injected launcher and `mkGuest` launcher accept the compact and legacy forms. That keeps cached boots compatible during the runtime-overlay rollout while reducing direct overlap on the shared guest bootstrap surface. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-core encode_egress_ca_cmdline_ --lib -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test -p mvm-build oci_runtime_inject -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo test --test nix_flake_structure mk_guest_accepts_compact_and_legacy_egress_ca_cmdline_tokens -- --test-threads=1`, `cargo clippy -p mvm-core --lib --tests -- -D warnings`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-build --lib --tests -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 fixed a concrete builder-rootfs `/init` defect and narrowed the remaining libkrun gap again.** The builder image behind the live libkrun proof was not only "silent under libkrun" — it had an invalid `/init` script on disk. A direct QEMU boot of a rebuilt builder cache on `88.99.197.234` first showed `Requested init /init failed (error -8)`, and inspecting `/init` from `rootfs.ext4` proved it started with a leading space before `#!/bin/sh`. `nix/lib/mk-guest.nix` now emits the shebang at byte 0, and `tests/nix_flake_structure.rs` pins that source contract directly. Rebuilding the cache through `mvmctl __builder-vm-bootstrap` now produces `/init` bytes starting with `#!/bin/sh`, and a serial QEMU boot of the exact libkrun-style disk layout reaches `mvm-host-vm-init`, mounts `/mvm/runtime` read-only from `/dev/vde`, and forks both `/mvm/runtime/agent` and `/mvm/runtime/egress-client`. A fresh trace-enabled timed libkrun witness against that rebuilt cache still times out with `networking=VsockDirect`, zero guest console/stdout bytes, and only libkrun device-wiring logs in `supervisor.stderr.log`, so the remaining Plan 242 open item is now libkrun-specific post-rootfs execution/handoff rather than a broken builder rootfs image.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now seals OCI block roots uniformly in the same worktree.** The runtime-lean OCI block-root path is no longer only "required-overlay" in metadata while still materializing a verity-free ext4 cache artifact. `mvm-build::run_image::materialize_run_rootfs` now always emits `rootfs.verity` + `rootfs.roothash`, and the shared `mvm-build::rootfs` layer writes those sidecars for both the default pure writer and the builder-VM fallback by hashing the finished `rootfs.ext4` bytes after materialization. That means `probe_verity_sidecar` now sees the same sealed block-root contract across Firecracker, qemu, and HVF block-backed OCI lanes, which is the prerequisite for treating those paths as honest required-overlay witnesses. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-build rootfs --lib --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-cli materialize_run_rootfs_default --lib --locked`, `cargo clippy -p mvm-build --lib --locked -- -D warnings`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-cli --lib --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now fail-closes libkrun workload egress at the backend boundary in the same worktree.** The current libkrun guest/runtime slice still shares more of the vsock contract than before, but the backend no longer pretends that is enough to satisfy the direct-path architecture. `LibkrunBackend::start` and `claim_standby` now refuse any workload whose resolved `NetworkPolicy` allows outbound egress, so an egress-enabled libkrun boot cannot silently continue onto the remaining non-vsock-proxy dataplane. Deny-all boots still work, which keeps the read-only runtime-overlay work moving without overstating libkrun networking. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-backend --lib validate_libkrun_network_policy --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-backend --lib libkrun_claim_standby_refuses_outbound_egress --locked`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-backend --lib --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now fail-closes explicit backend overrides for OCI egress in the same worktree.** The all-vsock direction is no longer just a best-effort auto-select rule. `mvm-cli` now refuses OCI image runs with outbound egress whenever the selected backend does not advertise `{vsock,no_guest_nic,host_vsock_proxy}`, and that guard is threaded through transient receipt generation plus persistent `machine start` / `machine run -d` preflight and receipt surfaces. An explicit `--hypervisor` / `MVM_HYPERVISOR` override therefore cannot silently steer an OCI egress workload back onto libkrun, Firecracker, or qemu and still get a signed preflight/receipt that pretends the contract is valid. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-cli --lib non_vsock_proxy_backend --locked` and `cargo clippy -p mvm-cli --tests --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 filled the missing HVF read-only proof harness in the same worktree.** The backend-specific proof matrix no longer has a raw-HVF-shaped harness gap while the OCI verity path remains separate work: `crates/mvm-backend/src/builder_runner/hvf_builder.rs` now exposes a narrow shell-job helper alongside the existing build path, and `live_hvf_builder_runtime_overlay_is_read_only` exists as an ignored macOS/Apple-Silicon witness. It uses the same proof body as the libkrun/qemu builder witnesses — record `/proc/cmdline`, require `/mvm/runtime` to appear read-only in `/proc/mounts`, and assert a `touch` under `/mvm/runtime` fails with `Read-only file system`. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-backend hvf_builder --lib --locked` and `cargo clippy -p mvm-backend --lib --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now pins the all-vsock OCI egress witness in the same worktree.** `tests/oci_image_runner_smoke.rs` no longer treats every backend as if it already satisfies the direct-path egress contract. A new `MVM_OCI_VSOCK_EGRESS_SMOKE=1` witness defaults to `hvf` on macOS, requires the selected backend to advertise `{vsock,no_guest_nic,host_vsock_proxy}`, and proves the guest-side contract by asserting both `mvm.vsock_egress=1` on `/proc/cmdline` and the injected `ALL_PROXY=socks5h://127.0.0.1:1080` env on a live `mvmctl run --image --allow-host ...` boot. That keeps the overlay rollout honest about the remaining libkrun gap: libkrun still has the shared guest/runtime contract pieces, but its workload dataplane is not yet an all-vsock witness and must not be counted as one. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test --test oci_image_runner_smoke --offline --locked` and `cargo clippy --test oci_image_runner_smoke --offline --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 libkrun workload lanes now speak the shared overlay/vsock guest contract in the same worktree.** The block-backed `libkrun` runtime-overlay work is no longer just an attach/layout change on the host side: secret-free workload runs with allowed egress now append `mvm.vsock_egress=1`, and the backend now spawns the same raw-vsock egress endpoint shape used by the other overlay-backed workload lanes, while deny-all/no-secret runs keep the endpoint defused. This removes one more libkrun-only divergence in the guest/runtime contract without overstating the remaining gap: the remaining libkrun workload dataplane is still not an all-vsock witness, and the live read-only overlay witness for libkrun remains open. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-backend libkrun_substitution_not_spawned_when_no_secrets_and_no_egress --lib`, `cargo test -p mvm-backend vsock_egress_cmdline_token_only_when_policy_allows_egress --lib`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-backend --lib -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 QEMU builder runtime-overlay proof is now green on the real KVM host.** The remaining builder-side required-overlay gap closed in the same worktree: `mvm-build::runtime_overlay` now treats the ext4 payload contents as part of the cache contract, rejecting stale overlays that are missing `/agent-dev-shell` or `/egress-client`, and the shared builder resolver now rebuilds the current overlay from the source checkout when that happens. The Linux fallback path also now drives `nix build --impure`, which is required because the runtime-overlay flake resolves `MVM_WORKSPACE_PATH` during evaluation. On `88.99.197.234`, the stale cache at `~/.cache/mvm/runtime-overlay/0.17.0/x86_64/overlay.ext4` was regenerated with `agent-dev-shell` and `egress-client`, and the ignored witness `cargo test -p mvm-build --features builder-vm --lib qemu_builder::vsock_module_tests::live_qemu_builder_runtime_overlay_is_read_only -- --ignored --exact --nocapture` then passed end-to-end. Local validation in the worktree: focused `runtime_overlay` resolver tests plus `cargo clippy -p mvm-build -p mvm-backend -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 portable dual-agent runtime overlay + real Firecracker required-overlay OCI witness LANDED in the same worktree.** The shared read-only runtime filesystem now stages portable relocated executables plus both overlay-shipped guest-agent variants (`/mvm/runtime/agent` and `/mvm/runtime/agent-dev-shell`), so the block-backed OCI required-overlay path no longer depends on rootfs-baked runtime binaries for either prod or dev-shell launches. The current Firecracker/KVM live proof on `88.99.197.234` is now green: the exact `oci_image_runner_smoke::run_image_block_root_required_overlay_is_read_only_on_selected_backend` witness passed end-to-end, proving `/mvm/runtime` is mounted read-only and the dev-shell OCI variant can exec through the overlay-backed agent while keeping the runtime/control path on the existing vsock-only seam. Follow-on work in `crates/mvm-cli/src/commands/vm/up.rs` now closes the ordinary cold-cache UX gap in a source-checkout-safe way too: plain required-overlay boots resolve the guest binaries on the host, seal the read-only overlay directly into `MVM_CACHE_DIR`, fall back to the signed/hash-verified release artifact only for installed builds, and still fail closed for explicitly pinned lifecycle boots whose recorded overlay version is absent. The same real-host proof now shows the source-checkout path populating both `runtime-overlay-bins/<version>/<arch>/...` and the sealed `runtime-overlay/<version>/<arch>/overlay.{ext4,verity,roothash}` before the Firecracker witness passes.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 gained a reusable live block-backed required-overlay witness in the same worktree.** The OCI live smoke no longer hard-codes the proof to Linux/Firecracker only: `tests/oci_image_runner_smoke.rs` now defaults to Firecracker on Linux and HVF elsewhere, accepts `MVM_OCI_IMAGE_RUNNER_HYPERVISOR=firecracker|hvf|libkrun|qemu`, and threads that through `MVM_HYPERVISOR` so the same guest-side `/mvm/runtime` read-only / `EROFS` assertions can validate the block-backed required-overlay path on multiple backend families. The earlier HVF exception is gone in the same worktree now that OCI block-root materialization always emits `rootfs.verity` + `rootfs.roothash`, so the HVF path no longer risks proving a virtiofs-root `RootfsOnly` boot when this witness asks for the block-backed required-overlay contract. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test --test oci_image_runner_smoke --offline --locked` and `cargo check --test oci_image_runner_smoke --offline --locked`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 OCI block roots now seal from a runtime-lean tree in the same worktree.** The OCI cache/materialize path no longer forces virtiofs-root and block-backed boots to share one injected runtime layout. A prepared `RootfsOnly` tree is kept for virtiofs-root OCI boots, but the cached block-backed ext4 is now built from a separate runtime-lean staging tree that omits baked `mvm-guest-agent`, `mvm-guest-netinit`, and `mvm-egress-client`. In the same slice, block-backed OCI boots moved from `PreferOverlay` to `RequiredOverlay`, and the OCI sidecar path now explicitly admits that runtime-lean required-overlay posture. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-build builder_vm --lib --offline --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-build oci_runtime_inject --lib --offline --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-cli runtime_source_policy_tests --lib --offline --locked`, `cargo check -p mvm-build -p mvm-cli --all-targets --offline --locked`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-build -p mvm-cli --all-targets --offline --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 `RootfsOnly` now means baked-runtime only in the same worktree.** The runtime-source policy label is no longer looser than the implementation on the remaining mixed launch paths: workload `/init`, OCI injected `/init`, and builder PID 1 now skip `/mvm/runtime/{netinit,egress-client,agent}` completely when `mvm.runtime_source_policy=rootfs_only`, while `prefer_overlay` keeps the compatibility overlay-first fallback and `required_overlay` remains fail-closed. This closes the policy-semantics gap for virtiofs-root OCI and other staged-rootfs consumers without claiming OCI injected-runtime retirement yet. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-build oci_runtime_inject --lib --offline --locked`, `cargo test -p mvm-build --bin mvm-host-vm-init --offline --locked`, `cargo check -p mvm-build --all-targets --offline --locked`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-build --all-targets --offline --locked -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 moved `mvm-egress-client` onto the runtime overlay in the same worktree.** The shared read-only guest runtime now covers one more named helper from the end-state list: `nix/images/runtime-overlay/flake.nix` stages `mvm-egress-client` at `/egress-client`, the workload `/init`, OCI `/init`, and builder PID 1 all prefer `/mvm/runtime/egress-client`, and required-overlay boots now fail closed if vsock-egress was requested but the overlay helper is missing. In the same slice, runtime-lean sealed `mkGuest` roots stop baking `/usr/local/bin/mvm-egress-client`, so the sealed rootfs/runtime split now includes the egress shim instead of leaving it as a rootfs exception. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-build oci_runtime_inject --lib`, `cargo test -p mvm-build --bin mvm-host-vm-init`, `cargo test --test nix_flake_structure`, `cargo check -p mvm-build --all-targets`, `cargo check -p mvmctl --test nix_flake_structure`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-build -p mvmctl --all-targets -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 now invalidates stale cached OCI roots for the new runtime layout in the same worktree.** The injected OCI cache contract now tracks the egress-client overlay move too: `OCI_RUNTIME_EPOCH` was bumped so a previously materialized injected rootfs that still bakes the old `mvm-egress-client` path is treated as stale and re-materializes before boot. That keeps the cache from silently bypassing the new overlay-first contract after the code moved on. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-cli stale_runtime_tag_marks_rootfs_not_current --lib`, `cargo check -p mvm-cli --all-targets`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-cli --all-targets -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 transient OCI block roots now advertise the real runtime-overlay contract in the same worktree.** The injected-root selector no longer hard-codes every OCI boot to `RootfsOnly`: virtiofs-root OCI launches still stay on the staged-rootfs runtime, while the first correction moved block-backed injected roots onto an overlay-backed contract instead of the old rootfs-only label. That intermediate fix closed the policy mismatch first; the later runtime-shape split in the same worktree then moved block-backed OCI fully onto a runtime-lean `RequiredOverlay` path. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-core select_runtime_source_policy --lib`, `cargo test -p mvm-cli runtime_source_policy_tests --lib`, `cargo test -p mvm-build oci_runtime_inject --lib`, `cargo check -p mvm-core -p mvm-cli -p mvm-build --all-targets`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-core -p mvm-cli -p mvm-build --all-targets -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-09 — Plan 242 stopped-vs-running runtime-overlay policy is now pinned in the same worktree.** The rollout's update model is no longer just a docs statement: the shared attach seam now proves two different contracts. Ordinary boots (`attach_runtime_overlay_if_cached`) resolve the overlay for the current host `mvmctl` version on the next start even if stale metadata exists, which keeps stopped-VM updates on the "update overlay, then restart" path. Lifecycle-pinned boots (`attach_runtime_overlay_if_cached_version`) still reuse the explicitly requested cached version and fail closed if that exact artifact is missing. In the same slice, `runtime_meta::VmRuntimeMeta` now documents `runtime_overlay_version` as observational boot metadata only, never a live-remount control knob for running VMs. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-cli runtime_overlay_attach_tests --lib`, `cargo test -p mvm-backend runtime_meta --lib`, `cargo check -p mvm-cli -p mvm-backend --all-targets`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-cli -p mvm-backend --all-targets -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-08 — Plan 242 QEMU builder lanes moved onto the same vsock egress contract in the same worktree.** The last builder-side slirp dependency is now gone: QEMU Stage 0, steady-state `run_build`, and builder shell jobs append `mvm.vsock_egress=1`, attach `vhost-vsock-pci`, and spawn the same raw `EGRESS_PORT` host endpoint under `NetworkPolicy::trusted_build_egress()` as the other builder backends. `stage0-init` no longer relies on slirp/static DNS for outbound fetches, so the builder/dev path is now aligned on the same no-NIC vsock egress shape across libkrun, HVF, and QEMU. Validation in the worktree: `cargo check -p mvm-build --features builder-vm --all-targets`, `cargo clippy -p mvm-build --features builder-vm --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test -p mvm-build qemu_builder --lib`, and `cargo test -p mvm-build --bin stage0-init`.

**Additional 2026-07-08 — Plan 242 QEMU builder overlay proof advanced on the real KVM host but remains open.** The host-side QEMU builder path now also attaches the cached runtime overlay as read-only `/dev/vdc`, and an ignored live witness (`qemu_builder::tests::live_qemu_builder_runtime_overlay_is_read_only`) exists to check `/mvm/runtime` directly. On `88.99.197.234` that proof now reaches the expected host cmdline (`mvm.runtime_source_policy=prefer_overlay mvm.runtime_data=/dev/vdc mvm.vsock_egress=1`) and the guest sees `virtio_blk ... [vdc]`, proving the attach path is being offered. The builder-image cache prerequisite is now closed in the source-checkout path: `ensure_builder_vm_image()` refuses stale manifests and can invoke the hidden `mvmctl __builder-vm-bootstrap` helper to populate an empty cache before the backend retries the load. Follow-on proof hardening (2026-07-09) tightened the remaining failure surface instead of guessing: the QEMU witness now records `/proc/cmdline` whenever `/mvm/runtime` is missing, and the libkrun builder resolver can auto-build `mvm-libkrun-supervisor` from the source checkout when the host has no matching binary preinstalled. The remaining live-proof blocker is therefore still the guest-side builder boot/finalization path, not cache population or missing harness prerequisites. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-build --features builder-vm ensure_builder_vm_image --lib`, `cargo test -p mvm-cli internal_builder_vm_bootstrap_command_is_hidden_but_parseable --lib`, `cargo check -p mvm-build -p mvm-cli --features builder-vm --all-targets`, `cargo clippy -p mvm-build --features builder-vm --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo clippy -p mvm-cli --all-targets -- -D warnings`, plus the live KVM witness runs described above.

**Additional 2026-07-08 — Plan 242 builder-image cache now fails closed on stale non-vsock/non-overlay manifests in the same worktree.** The shared `ensure_builder_vm_image()` gate no longer accepts any cached `vmlinux`/`rootfs.ext4` pair that merely exists on disk: it now requires `manifest.json` to declare `runtime_overlay_ready=true` and `vsock_egress_ready=true`, and otherwise refuses the cache with a re-bootstrap instruction before libkrun, HVF, or QEMU can boot it. The builder-vm flake now emits those manifest fields for both the steady-state builder image and the Stage 0 rootfs, so a refreshed cache advertises the overlay/vsock-only contract explicitly instead of relying on implicit rootfs contents. Validation in the worktree: `cargo test -p mvm-build --features builder-vm ensure_builder_vm_image --lib`, `cargo check -p mvm-build --features builder-vm --all-targets`, and `cargo clippy -p mvm-build --features builder-vm --all-targets -- -D warnings`.

**Additional 2026-07-08 — Plan 242 builder vsock-only steady-state relay landed in the same worktree.** The builder guest-side groundwork is now paired with the host half for the steady-state lanes: rootfs-backed libkrun builder boots append `mvm.vsock_egress=1`, expose `EGRESS_PORT` as a host-listen vsock channel, and spawn a per-VM raw egress endpoint under `NetworkPolicy::trusted_build_egress()` instead of relying on a legacy guest-network helper; the trusted HVF builder runner now threads the same `EGRESS_PORT` relay socket and raw endpoint into its builder spec, so builders stop being a special no-relay path on that backend. That closes the remaining "version-matched, not latest" proof gap for lifecycle-driven fresh boots while `vm restore` remains intentionally unavailable on current backends. # Refactor status — rollup checklist

**Additional 2026-07-11 — Plan 236 OCI prod witness is now green on the closeout branch.** The active `feat/plan-236-oci-seal-closeout` lane no longer stops at "the dm-verity seal fallback exists in code." The macOS source-checkout witness now forces `--kernel-source compile`, resolves the runtime-selected manifest digest before checking cache outputs, and passes end to end on a signed Chainguard BusyBox digest while proving the OCI cache contains real `rootfs.verity` + `rootfs.roothash` sidecars. The same live run exposed one more real Stage 0 failure mode: a matching persistent seed-store marker could still point at an incomplete reused runtime and die later with missing `bin/nix` or CA bundle content. `stage0-init` now validates that reused seed runtime and re-seeds automatically when it is incomplete. Validation is green with `cargo check --workspace`, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, `cargo test --workspace`, the focused `mvm-build` shell-context/Stage-0 coverage, and the live `MVM_OCI_IMAGE_RUNNER_PROD_SMOKE=1` witness.

**Additional 2026-07-11 — Plan 236 Phase 2A egress data plane LANDED (five merged PRs).** The tunnel is no longer pre-forwarding: an admitted workload now actually reaches the network over the vsock packet tunnel, gated per-flow by the admitted `NetworkPolicy`/`DnsPinRegistry` and audited, with no guest NIC, no gvproxy/passt, and no TLS MITM. Linux forwards raw L3 through a host `/dev/net/tun` + kernel masquerade NAT; DNS needs no live resolver (the host injects the admission pins as guest `/etc/hosts`); the production caller populates `VmStartConfig.network_tunnel` for admitted `no_routable_guest_nic` boots (deny-all stays `DropAll`, fail-closed). macOS has no unprivileged kernel NAT, so it forwards in a userspace `smoltcp` stack bridged to unprivileged host sockets behind the same worker/gate seam — TCP (#1639), UDP (#1647), ICMP echo (#1650) — closing the original `machine run --allow-host google.com … ping google.com` on macOS end to end, no root. Linux raw-L3 data plane is #1634; the untrusted gate packet-parser is fuzzed (#1643). Plan 236 Phase 2A T1–T6 ticked. **Remaining honest gap:** no live workload egress run yet (needs a Linux + libkrun host; none available), so the three Phase 2A "live workload witness" boxes stay unchecked. All five PRs verified locally against the full CI gate set (nightly rustfmt, workspace + Linux clippy, all xtask gates, nextest, Linux cross-build — CI's Lint/Test run on Linux and never exercise the `#[cfg(macos)]` code) and merged via the queue.

**Additional 2026-07-11 — Plan 237 Phase 0 validation follow-up, clean baseline still pending.** Fresh worktree validation is green for the target `mvm-oci-init` musl zigbuild, the full `mvm-guest` test suite, focused stale-cache/rematerialization `mvm-cli` tests, and the live Alpine `ps aux` smoke from isolated `/tmp` state after seeding the isolated cache with the already-built workload kernel. The density harness now refuses artifact/data directories whose generated HVF agent socket path would exceed the macOS Unix socket limit, so long paths fail before expensive prepull/build work. The checked baseline report remains open: the long-path 1/5/10/25 attempt cleaned waves 1, 5, 10, and failed wave 25 with no matching processes left, but wave 25 was invalid due HVF agent socket path failures; the short-path rerun was aborted before measurement when unrelated Rust builds started during prepull, triggering the density-run stop condition again.

**Additional 2026-07-10 / 2026-07-11 — Plan 236 restore-time replay residual closed on branch `fix/plan-236-plan219-prod-ready-main`.** Host-signed `PostRestore` grant refreshes now bind to the currently pinned VM lineage instead of trusting any valid host-signed sibling grant: the wire envelope carries predecessor `session_id` + plan nonce, the guest refuses re-pin when that lineage does not match the currently pinned grant, and the Firecracker fork path stamps the predecessor fields from the parent VM's admitted grant before delivery. Validation is green with focused `mvm-guest`, `mvm-core`, and `mvm-cli` tests, `cargo clippy --workspace --all-targets -- -D warnings`, and a host `cargo test --workspace` rerun. The old production/verity OCI seal-path blocker is now closed on `feat/plan-236-oci-seal-closeout`; the remaining Plan 236 blocker is the separate macOS verb-grant witness.

It now also scans the new raw host-authority tunnel seams (`mvm-backend` tunnel-worker spawn, guest tunnel/TUN code, and host tunnel/TUN code), so those runtime files fail closed if a future change reintroduces guest-NIC or legacy-helper tokens such as `virtio-net`, `passt`, or `gvproxy`. The xtask also now has direct unit coverage for mixed file/directory guarded paths and for comment-skipping behavior, so this Phase 2C gate widening is tested rather than declarative. The mapping from an already-selected builder backend to the concrete Stage 0 driver now lives in one helper with a unit test that proves the real post-cutover contract directly: `hvf` still lowers to libkrun until an HVF-specific Stage 0 exists, explicit qemu stays qemu, and Linux auto libkrun does not silently widen into a qemu retry. The sibling `dev_build` single-shot comment now matches that selector instead of describing the pre-cutover libkrun→qemu retry story. It now states the real shared policy instead: auto libkrun stays libkrun-only, qemu remains explicit dev/test-only, and the only automatic builder handoff still admitted is HVF→libkrun. The local `builder_backend_attempt_order` tests in `mvm-cli` now pin those invariants directly so the CLI-facing bootstrap path cannot drift from `mvm-build`'s shared selector by narrative or by behavior.
